> You’re an operative stuck in a time loop, always brought back from the dead to the same point. > Bellum is the next evolution of platoon-scale tactical shooters, built in Unreal Engine 5 and shaped by die-hard fans who demanded more from the genre. > In Bloody Hell Hotel, you embody a vampire. You wake up from a coma that has lasted for centuries, only to find your once-grand estate is now in ruins. It's time to turn it into a prosperous hotel! It's no easy task, though, as times are tough and resources are scarce. There are shortages of food, supplies, and workforce. Being a blood-thirsty monster doesn't help with customer satisfaction either. Luckily, underneath the estate lies a crypt and a system of caves. By foraging, farming, crafting, and fighting your way through the monster-infested dungeons, you'll find the resources you need to restore greatness to your mansion and turn it into a thriving hotel business.

### Call of Duty: Modern Warfare 4

<img src="https://images.igdb.com/igdb/image/upload/t_cover_big/coc4mu.jpg" alt="Call of Duty: Modern Warfare 4 cover" width="200">

**Platforms:** Windows, Nintendo Switch 2, PlayStation 5, Xbox Series X/S &nbsp;|&nbsp; **Developer:** Infinity Ward &nbsp;|&nbsp; **Publisher:** Activision &nbsp;|&nbsp; **Genre:** FPS &nbsp;|&nbsp; **Released:** October 23

> Call of Duty: Modern Warfare 4 is a first-person shooter and the follow-up to Modern Warfare III (2023), continuing the rebooted Modern Warfare storyline. The campaign is set during a North Korean invasion of South Korea and features dual perspectives: a young South Korean soldier named Private Park facing his first combat deployment, and Captain Price operating outside official channels on a personal mission. The game also includes multiplayer with 6v6 core maps, large-scale vehicle combat maps, and a dynamic map system called Kill Block that reshapes layouts between rounds. A DMZ extraction mode is also featured. The game is released exclusively on current-generation platforms and PC.
>
> _Synopsis source: IGDB_

[IGDB](https://www.igdb.com/games/call-of-duty-modern-warfare-4)

### Dragon Quest VII Reimagined

<img src="https://images.igdb.com/igdb/image/upload/t_cover_big/cobkan.jpg" alt="Dragon Quest VII Reimagined cover" width="200">

**Platforms:** Windows, Nintendo Switch, Nintendo Switch 2, PlayStation 5, Xbox Series X/S &nbsp;|&nbsp; **Developer:** Square Enix &nbsp;|&nbsp; **Publisher:** Square Enix &nbsp;|&nbsp; **Genre:** RPG &nbsp;|&nbsp; **Released:** February 5

> DRAGON QUEST VII Reimagined is a modern remake of DRAGON QUEST VII, which originally launched on PlayStation in 2000. In this new reimagined version of the game, the core concept of the original title remains, but the battle mechanics, graphics and story have all been updated and improved, providing players with a more streamlined and focused experience. In particular, the graphics in the game were completely overhauled from the original, using real-life doll figures for the characters and monsters that were scanned into the game to create stunning hand-crafted visuals. This definitive version of the game is perfect for fans who want to revisit the beloved story or newcomers who are diving into the world for the first time.
>
> _Synopsis source: IGDB_

[Wikipedia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dragon_Quest_VII_Reimagined) · [IGDB](https://www.igdb.com/games/dragon-quest-vii-reimagined)

### Gamblers Table

<img src="https://images.igdb.com/igdb/image/upload/t_cover_big/co9s0k.jpg" alt="Gamblers Table cover" width="200">

**Platforms:** Windows, Linux &nbsp;|&nbsp; **Developer:** greenpixels &nbsp;|&nbsp; **Publisher:** greenpixels &nbsp;|&nbsp; **Genre:** Point-and-click, Simulator, Strategy, Indie &nbsp;|&nbsp; **Released:** January 11, 2026

> Gamblers Table is an idle automation game where you toss coins to earn money. Buy bigger and better coins, unlock upgrades and hire little helpers to automate the process and take care of all the busy flipping work for you. Simple. Satisfying.  Click a coin to toss it. Every time it lands on Heads, you earn money.  The more coins you own, the more you can flip. Buy upgrades to make them flip faster and yield more money. Discover curious interactions and unexpected synergies between different coin types.  Hire little helpers to take care of all the manual labour for you. They don’t complain, and they never question.  Leave your fortune behind and rise to something greater. Start again with peculiar new talents you didn’t have before.  Admire your riches, lean back, and watch your automated coin-flipping empire grow.
>
> _Synopsis source: IGDB_

[IGDB](https://www.igdb.com/games/gamblers-table)

### Gothic 1 Remake

<img src="https://images.igdb.com/igdb/image/upload/t_cover_big/co9lfu.jpg" alt="Gothic 1 Remake cover" width="200">

**Platforms:** Windows, PlayStation 5, Xbox Series X/S &nbsp;|&nbsp; **Developer:** Alkimia Interactive &nbsp;|&nbsp; **Publisher:** THQ Nordic &nbsp;|&nbsp; **Genre:** Action RPG &nbsp;|&nbsp; **Released:** June 5

> Gothic 1 Remake is an action role-playing game set in a third-person perspective within a fantasy open world. It is a remake of the 2001 game Gothic, developed by Alkimia Interactive using Unreal Engine 5. The player controls a nameless prisoner sent into the Valley of Mines, a penal colony surrounded by a magical barrier. The game features NPC routines, faction-based quest paths, and a progression system where the player starts with no skills and must learn them from trainers. Navigation is done without a minimap, and the game world is 10 to 30 percent larger than the original, with expanded chapters and additional quest content. Combat and skill systems are based on the original game's design.

> Island of Winds takes you to 17th century Iceland, plunging them into a magical world teeming with trolls, giant spiders, and mythical creatures. Experience all of this through the eyes of the magic-wielding Brynhild, a member of the Balance Keepers who draws her magical power from the natural world.  Brynhild's journey has her on a quest to find her mentor, Hrymja, that will take her through nine incredible landscapes that draw on Iceland's stunning natural beauty. These locations range from the fiery Lava of Skjól to the treacherous Jökla lagoon and gleaming Obsidian Shore. Brynhild will face legendary creatures on her journey, such as the powerful and majestic Bear King, Hafgúfa - Guardian of the Sea and the majestic elven lady Vör of the Oasis.

### Lost Host

<img src="https://images.igdb.com/igdb/image/upload/t_cover_big/cob3bs.jpg" alt="Lost Host cover" width="200">

**Platforms:** Windows &nbsp;|&nbsp; **Developer:** Gest.LT &nbsp;|&nbsp; **Publisher:** Gest.LT &nbsp;|&nbsp; **Genre:** Adventure, Indie &nbsp;|&nbsp; **Expected:** November 4, 2026

> Lost Host is a story-driven adventure puzzle game about a small toy car searching for its missing owner. When the boy mysteriously disappears, the toy car begins a journey through the house and beyond, exploring dark rooms, hallways, foggy streets, and abandoned places while following clues to uncover what happened.  Players control the toy car as it navigates dangerous environments, solves physics-based puzzles, and interacts with objects to progress. The world is full of hazards for a tiny toy car - heights, fire, water - as well as enemies such as dogs, robotic vacuums, and hostile toy creatures.  The game combines atmospheric exploration, light horror elements, and emotional storytelling, creating a family-friendly adventure centered on mystery, discovery, and survival in a large, human-sized world.
>
> _Synopsis source: IGDB_

[IGDB](https://www.igdb.com/games/lost-host)

> MIO: Memories in Orbit is a 2.5D side-scrolling metroidvania platformer. Players control a robot named Mio who explores a vast spaceship called the Vessel, gaining new abilities such as wall climbing, dodging, grappling, and gliding while fighting machines that have gone rogue. Combat emphasizes aerial fighting, and players can equip modifiers that adjust their abilities at the cost of modifier slots. Currency called Nacre is used to purchase upgrades and is lost on death, and fast-travel devices serve as save points across the ship.

> Pathologic 3 is a psychological horror game in which you play Daniil Dankovsky, a rationalist doctor trying to defeat death itself as the same doomed town collapses around him. It is a standalone follow-up to Pathologic 2 and a reimagining of the Bachelor route from the original Pathologic, rather than a direct sequel or prequel. Its major new distinction is time manipulation: the premise remains a 12-day plague crisis, but the Bachelor can revisit and alter key events, reflecting his obsession with control, causality, and conquering death.
>
> _Synopsis source: IGDB_

> Pokémon Infinite Fusion is a Pokémon RPG fangame based on the Pokémon fusion generator trend, featuring sprite art from a large community of artists. In this version of Pokémon, Silph Co. has created a DNA splicer that can work on any Pokemon, allowing their stats, visual appearance, and typing to be combined alongside their available abilities and moves. The game includes both the Kanto and Johto regions, and includes over 570 Pokémon, including the first 386 Pokémon from the first three generations alongside a suite of specifically chosen Pokémon from later generations.  With hundreds of thousands of fusions available, even Professor Oak doesn't expect you to "catch 'em all"!
>
> _Synopsis source: IGDB_

### Romeo is a Dead Man

<img src="https://images.igdb.com/igdb/image/upload/t_cover_big/coakmt.jpg" alt="Romeo is a Dead Man cover" width="200">

**Platforms:** Windows, PlayStation 5, Xbox Series X/S &nbsp;|&nbsp; **Developer:** Grasshopper Manufacture &nbsp;|&nbsp; **Publisher:** Grasshopper Manufacture &nbsp;|&nbsp; **Genre:** Action-adventure &nbsp;|&nbsp; **Released:** February 11

> Latest ultra-violent sci-fi action title from Grasshopper Manufacture and Suda51.  Step into the blood-soaked boots of Romeo Stargazer, a man pulled back from the brink of death by a time paradox gone wrong. Now reborn as Agent Dead Man of the FBI Space-Time Division, and armed with the mysterious Dead Gear mask, Romeo must chase the universe’s most dangerous fugitives across alternate realities.  Blending high-octane gunplay, brutal sword combat, and over-the-top sci-fi insanity, Romeo Is a Dead Man is GhM’s bloodiest action game yet. Players can expect mind-bending twists, outrageous gimmicks, and a deeply cathartic combat system that turns enemies’ powers against them in universe-shattering battles.  As Romeo tears through the multiverse, he’s also on a desperate quest to find Juliet, his mysteriously vanished girlfriend. With a narrative that fuses love, loss, and metaphysical chaos, Romeo Is a Dead Man offers a stylish and unpredictable journey through the very fabric of time itself.  Can you survive the bleeding edge of space-time? Or will you become just another ghost in the paradox?
>
> _Synopsis source: IGDB_

[Wikipedia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Romeo_is_a_Dead_Man) · [IGDB](https://www.igdb.com/games/romeo-is-a-dead-man)

> The Bornless is an FPS action shooter packed with a variety of gameplay modes. Dive into last-man-standing co-op gameplay or try the thrilling demon versus disciple mode, all within a terror-filled world. Players pair up and form teams, conjure demons to aid in combat, and utilize strategic planning and resource management to survive. Whether you choose an aggressive playstyle or prefer a more cautious approach, the choice is yours.  Join or create a Faction and amass various resources to upgrade and enhance your Safehouse or personalize your gear to give a new look to your arsenal as you go to war with other Factions.  You will also experience deep lore and storyline involving all the characters within The Bornless universe, which will be revealed as facts are discovered by players.
>

### Valheim

<img src="https://images.igdb.com/igdb/image/upload/t_cover_big/cocygu.jpg" alt="Valheim cover" width="200">

**Platforms:** Windows, macOS, Linux, Nintendo Switch 2, PlayStation 5, Xbox One, Xbox Series X/S &nbsp;|&nbsp; **Developer:** Iron Gate Studio &nbsp;|&nbsp; **Publisher:** Coffee Stain Publishing &nbsp;|&nbsp; **Genre:** Sandbox, Survival &nbsp;|&nbsp; **Released:** September 9

> Valheim is an open-world survival and exploration game set in a procedurally generated world inspired by Norse mythology. Playing alone or with up to nine other players, you explore dangerous biomes, gather resources, craft weapons and armour, build Viking settlements and longships, and battle powerful creatures and bosses in order to prove yourself worthy of Odin.  Progression is tied to exploration and defeating the Forsaken, with each biome introducing new enemies, resources, crafting possibilities and challenges. From the peaceful Meadows to the frozen Deep North, players can build, farm, sail, fight and explore the tenth world at their own pace.
>
> _Synopsis source: IGDB_
